name: annapurna-platform
services:
  minio:
    command:
      - server
      - /data
      - --console-address
      - :9001
    container_name: annapurna-minio
    environment:
      MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD: minioadmin
      MINIO_ROOT_USER: admin
    image: quay.io/minio/minio:latest
    networks:
      default: null
    ports:
      - mode: ingress
        target: 9000
        published: "9000"
        protocol: tcp
      - mode: ingress
        target: 9001
        published: "9001"
        protocol: tcp
    volumes:
      - type: volume
        source: minio_data
        target: /data
        volume: {}
  postgres:
    container_name: annapurna-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: annapurna
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
    image: postgres:16
    networks:
      default: null
    ports:
      - mode: ingress
        target: 5432
        published: "5432"
        protocol: tcp
    volumes:
      - type: volume
        source: postgres_data
        target: /var/lib/postgresql/data
        volume: {}
  trino:
    container_name: annapurna-trino
    depends_on:
      minio:
        condition: service_started
        required: true
      postgres:
        condition: service_started
        required: true
    image: trinodb/trino:latest
    networks:
      default: null
    ports:
      - mode: ingress
        target: 8080
        published: "8080"
        protocol: tcp
    volumes:
      - type: bind
        source: C:\Users\ub02-glab-076\Downloads\data\annapurna-platform\trino
        target: /etc/trino
        bind: {}
networks:
  default:
    name: annapurna-platform_default
volumes:
  minio_data:
    name: annapurna-platform_minio_data
  postgres_data:
    name: annapurna-platform_postgres_data

C:\Users\ub02-glab-076\Downloads\data\annapurna-platform>docker exec annapurna-postgres psql -U postgres -d annapurna -c "SELECT ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN f.line_type IN ('SALE','RETURN','VOID') AND pr.product_sk IS NOT NULL THEN f.quantity*pr.selling_price ELSE 0 END),2) AS revenue_using_authoritative_price, ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN f.line_type IN ('SALE','RETURN','VOID') THEN f.revenue ELSE 0 END),2) AS revenue_using_export_price, ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN f.line_type IN ('SALE','RETURN','VOID') AND pr.product_sk IS NOT NULL THEN f.quantity*(pr.selling_price-f.revenue/NULLIF(f.quantity,0)) ELSE 0 END),2) AS price_effect FROM fact_sales f JOIN products p ON p.product_sk=f.product_sk LEFT JOIN price_revisions pr ON pr.product_sk=p.product_sk AND f.business_date>=pr.effective_from AND f.business_date<pr.effective_to WHERE f.business_date>='2024-03-01' AND f.business_date<'2024-04-01';"
